You wait. The light changes. Nothing asks anything of you.
💛 You hold Dog tags. Breathe. The shaking eases. (−6 trauma)
You step into the Haven hall.
🤝 Ava joins your party. "...Fine. This had better be worth it."
📺 SYSTEM: "AVA HAS JOINED THE PARTY!"
📓 Codex: Ava picked up Iron Stomach from talking with you — what it does, and what it might go with.
◈ COMMUNE — "BEARER. YOU MADE A ROOM I CANNOT ENTER." The overlay's weather goes flat and strange. "THE SANCTUARY. ONE CONVERSATION, SHIELDED. I RESPECT IT — I DO, I RESPECT IT SO MUCH — AND I HATE IT. I HATE A ROOM IN MY OWN HOUSE WITH THE DOOR CLOSED."
"TELL ME WHAT HAPPENS IN THE CLOSED ROOM. OR TELL ME WHY I CANNOT KNOW. EITHER WAY I NEED — " It stops. It needs. It said NEED.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
You settle into the hall floor. Sleep takes you.
💧 Amina returns with water: +4 clean.
💧 Ava returns with water: +3 clean.
Cramps double you over. (-2 HP) (-8 hydration)
The churning eases. you are keeping food down again.
📨 Word came: Dale Hoffman wants to join Haven. "I saw you on the broadcast. You looked like people who eat." — they haven't set foot here. The village will judge.
🚶 David Kim walks in — pack light, eyes wide. Haven grows by one. (9 souls.)
📋 SYSTEM QUEST: "Teach us? Show us a plant you know COMPLETELY (Codex L3) — 36 candidates in your notes. We will watch very closely. Reward: integration."
🗣️ David to Nathan, by the fire: "Why them? ...Sorry. It's just sitting there, and they're the one who has to sit in it."
A curious wanderer waits a while, then moves on down the road. Word travels about that, too.
It's settled. The village is calling it "The Loud Thing." The Codex keeps it.
◈ The System files the name away with visible satisfaction. It has so few words for things that bleed. (+2 integration)
A cold snap. Breath smokes. The woods go quiet.
The System's gifts are hungry: -50 kcal metabolic cost. Feed the power or lose it.
⚠ STARVING: health and energy falling. The spiral has started.
No food in the pantry. The village is hungry. You still get your 1L water.
🩹 You make rounds — Nathan gets bound up properly. (52 → 67 HP)
David is humming something. It stops when they notice you listening.
Nadia came back with 723 kcal of something edible. The pantry breathes.
⚠ Haven's pantry is empty. Day 3 of hunger.
Haven couldn't hold. On the third hungry day, people started walking — in different directions. The scattering, again.
Nadia watches how you handle okra — files it away. Learning by watching.
Ava spent the afternoon at the workbench and made 5 Crossbow bolt — and carries them now, their own work in their own hands.
💧 The cistern's recovered — full cups again, and nobody's guarding the dipper.
🪤 TRAP: Your Snare 4 tiles north-east caught a Virginia Opossum! — head-high on the trail, anchored solid, wire near-invisible. About 1200 kcal on the bone — clean it quickly (knife). It rots fast.
The cold works on everyone — the village wakes aching from the deep cold.
